a:null
c:{"metadata":[["$","title","0",{"children":"Doing many things at once in Go: from one go keyword to a worker pool  | jialinhuang"}],["$","meta","1",{"name":"description","content":"Start by putting go in front of a function, then add one tool per problem it runs into, WaitGroup to wait, channels to bring results back, mutex for shared variables, select and context for timeouts and cancellation, worker pools and errgroup to limit how many run at once. Ends with the panics and deadlocks you hit when it goes wrong."}],["$","link","2",{"rel":"canonical","href":"/v2/go-goroutines-waitgroup-to-deadlock"}]],"error":null,"digest":"$undefined"}
11:"$c:metadata"
13:I[86734,["7654","static/chunks/7654-2fe46cddc56d2856.js","9489","static/chunks/app/v2/%5Bslug%5D/page-2e406f5eec6a5c88.js"],"MdDoc"]
14:T1a9c9,<p>The simplest way to make Go do many things at once is to put <code>go</code> in front of a function call. That is where the problems start, though: <code>main</code> doesn't wait for it, the result can't come back, and when several goroutines change the same variable, the count comes out wrong.</p>
<p>One scene runs through the whole article: a restaurant kitchen.</p>





















































<table><thead><tr><th>Kitchen</th><th>Go</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>a cook</td><td>a goroutine</td></tr><tr><td>the head chef, who locks up and turns off the lights when leaving</td><td><code>main</code> (when <code>main</code> returns, the program ends)</td></tr><tr><td>the counter on the whiteboard: a tick for every dish handed out, crossed off when it is done</td><td><code>sync.WaitGroup</code></td></tr><tr><td>the pass, the shelf where cooks put finished plates</td><td>a channel</td></tr><tr><td>the "kitchen closed" sign on the pass</td><td><code>close(ch)</code></td></tr><tr><td>one pen, one wok: while someone uses it, everyone else waits</td><td><code>sync.Mutex</code></td></tr><tr><td>watching the pass and the kitchen timer at once, and dealing with whichever moves first</td><td><code>select</code></td></tr><tr><td>the front of house calls in "the customer cancelled", and every cook stops</td><td><code>context</code></td></tr><tr><td>only three burners, so no matter how many dishes, only three cook at once</td><td>a worker pool</td></tr><tr><td>one dish burns, the whole table is redone, and the other cooks can stop</td><td><code>errgroup</code></td></tr><tr><td>the kitchen manager: when every cook is waiting on someone else and nobody can move, he closes the kitchen and writes down what each cook was waiting for</td><td>the runtime's deadlock detector (prints <code>fatal error</code> and where each goroutine is stuck, then ends the program)</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>All programs below were run on Go 1.26.1 (darwin/arm64); errgroup is <code>golang.org/x/sync</code> v0.20.0.</p>
<h2>Part 1: running things at the same time</h2>
<h3>The naive version</h3>
<p>Start with the simplest case. Two tasks, one after the other, with sleep standing in for real work.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Doing something important..."</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Doing something else, equally important..."</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">	doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">	doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>It takes 5 seconds, because the second task waits for the first to finish. One cook, two dishes: finish the first, then start the second.</p>
<h3>"I'll just put go in front of everything"</h3>
<p>A goroutine is a function the Go runtime runs concurrently, and starting one is much cheaper than starting an OS thread. So the natural next step is:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"I'm done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<pre><code>$ go run main.go
I'm done
</code></pre>
<p>Out of 1000 runs, 911 printed only <code>I'm done</code>. The other 89 printed a line or two more, but neither task finished its sleep.</p>
<p>In the kitchen, the head chef hands out two dishes, says "I'm done", turns off the lights and leaves, and the cooks go home with him wherever they were. When <code>main</code> returns, the program ends, and every goroutine still running ends with it.</p>
<p>So how does <code>main</code> wait for the goroutines to finish?</p>
<h3>WaitGroup: the counter on the whiteboard</h3>
<p><code>sync.WaitGroup</code> is a counter. The head chef puts a tick on the whiteboard for every dish handed out, and each cook crosses one off when done. Until every tick is crossed off, the head chef cannot leave.</p>
<ul>
<li><code>wg.Add(1)</code>: add one, one more task.</li>
<li><code>wg.Done()</code>: subtract one, this task is finished.</li>
<li><code>wg.Wait()</code>: wait until the counter reaches 0.</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">id</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">wg</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Worker </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> starting</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Worker </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(i, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"All workers done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Five workers, each sleeping 1 second, finish in 1.0 second total.</p>
<p>Write <code>Done</code> with <code>defer</code>. Then the counter goes down however the worker returns. Forget it, and one tick stays on the board forever, so <code>wg.Wait()</code> waits forever.</p>
<p>Since Go 1.25 you can also use <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/sync#WaitGroup.Go"><code>wg.Go(f)</code></a>, which does the add, the <code>go</code>, and the done in one call. Most existing code still uses the form above.</p>
<h3>Channels: when goroutines need to send back results</h3>
<p>A WaitGroup tells you <em>when</em> everyone is finished, not <em>what</em> they produced. For results you need a channel, which is the pass: cooks put plates on it, someone takes them off the other side. <code>make(chan int, 3)</code> is a pass with 3 slots. When all slots are full, a cook stands there holding the plate.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">id</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">wg</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">result</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> chan&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> id </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(i, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg, result)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">		close</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(result)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> r </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> range</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> result {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Received: </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, r)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"All workers done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>This runs fine. The small <code>go func()</code> in the middle has two lines: <code>wg.Wait()</code> waits for every cook, and <code>close(result)</code> hangs the "kitchen closed" sign.</p>
<p><code>close</code> does not delete or empty the channel. After the sign goes up, nobody can put a new plate on the pass, but plates already there can still be taken. So the <code>for range</code> takes the remaining plates and stops once the channel is empty.</p>
<p>Why are those two lines wrapped in <code>go func()</code> instead of sitting directly in <code>main</code>?</p>
<h3>The first deadlock</h3>
<p>Two things keep this program from getting stuck:</p>
<ul>
<li><strong>The <code>go func()</code></strong>: it lets <code>main</code> go straight to the <code>for range</code>, taking plates off while cooks put them on, so the pass is rarely full.</li>
<li><strong>A big enough pass</strong>: 3 slots for 3 workers, so every plate fits even if nobody takes one.</li>
</ul>
<p>Keep either one and the program finishes:</p>








































<table><thead><tr><th><code>go func()</code>?</th><th>buffer size</th><th>result</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>yes</td><td>3</td><td>finishes</td></tr><tr><td>yes</td><td>2</td><td>finishes</td></tr><tr><td>yes</td><td>0 (unbuffered)</td><td>finishes</td></tr><tr><td>no</td><td>3</td><td>finishes</td></tr><tr><td>no</td><td>2</td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td></tr><tr><td>no</td><td>0 (unbuffered)</td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Remove both:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// 3 senders, only 2 slots</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()                     </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// main blocks here instead of draining</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">close</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(result)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> r </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> range</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> result { </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">...</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> }</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>This is how it gets stuck:</p>
<ol>
<li>The pass holds 2 plates. The third cook finds it full and stands at <code>result &#x3C;- ...</code> holding the plate.</li>
<li>He never reaches <code>defer wg.Done()</code>, so his tick is never crossed off.</li>
<li>The head chef keeps standing at <code>wg.Wait()</code>.</li>
<li>The only one who takes plates off the pass is the head chef, and he never reaches the <code>for range</code>.</li>
<li>Nobody takes a plate, no slot opens, and the third cook keeps standing. Back to step 1.</li>
</ol>
<p>Everyone is waiting on someone else. The runtime sees that every goroutine is stuck, prints <code>all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code>, and ends the program.</p>
<p>The fix is to put the <code>go func()</code> back. Then <code>main</code> starts taking plates right away, and the buffer size stops mattering. A buffer is hard to size to the workload, while taking plates as they arrive always works, so the <code>go func()</code> is the safer of the two.</p>
<h3>Mutex: several cooks updating one number</h3>
<p>On the kitchen wall is a count of dishes served today. Each cook, after finishing a dish, walks over and adds one. In Go, that is 1000 goroutines each doing <code>orders++</code>:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">orders </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 0</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 0</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1000</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		orders</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(orders)</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>It should end at 1000. Over 30 runs, the result was between 935 and 983, and never 1000.</p>
<p><code>orders++</code> looks like one step but is three: read the current value, add one, write it back. If two cooks both read 41, each adds one and both write 42, and one dish goes uncounted. This is a data race. Run it with <code>go run -race</code> and it prints <code>WARNING: DATA RACE</code>, plus the two lines that touched the same variable.</p>
<p>The fix is a <code>sync.Mutex</code>. After one goroutine calls <code>Lock()</code>, anyone else calling <code>Lock()</code> waits until the holder calls <code>Unlock()</code>. It is like having one pen by the wall: only the cook holding the pen can change the number.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> mu </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Mutex</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	mu.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	orders</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	mu.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}()</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>With the lock, 10 runs all printed 1000. If all you need is to add to a number, <code>atomic.Int64</code> from <code>sync/atomic</code> also works: call <code>Add(1)</code>, no lock needed.</p>
<h2>Part 2: waiting, cancelling, limiting</h2>
<h3>select: waiting on several things at once</h3>
<p>So far <code>main</code> waits on one thing at a time: <code>wg.Wait()</code> for the counter to reach zero, <code>&#x3C;-result</code> for a plate on the pass. Sometimes you need to wait on two things and handle whichever comes first. The most common case is a timeout: the customer waits at most 1 second, and if the dish isn't ready, someone tells them.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> cook</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> time</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Duration</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> string</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> string</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(d)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> "steak"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> ch</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	select</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> dish </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">cook</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Second):</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"got"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, dish)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">After</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Second):</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"timeout"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>select</code> blocks until one of its cases has a value. The steak takes 2 seconds and <code>time.After(1 * time.Second)</code> sends a value after 1 second, so this prints <code>timeout</code> after 1.0 seconds.</p>
<p>The channel in <code>cook</code> has 1 slot because nobody will take the steak after the timeout. With no slot, that cook would stand holding the plate forever, and the goroutine would never end.</p>
<p><code>select</code> can also have a <code>default</code>. If no channel is ready, it takes <code>default</code> and doesn't wait.</p>
<h3>context: the customer cancelled</h3>
<p>A timeout stops waiting when time runs out. Sometimes the signal comes from outside instead, "no need to finish": the user closes the page and the HTTP request is cancelled. Cooks still working on it should stop too, or the work is wasted.</p>
<p><code>context</code> carries that message. <code>context.WithCancel</code> gives you a <code>ctx</code> and a <code>cancel</code> function. After <code>cancel()</code>, the <code>ctx.Done()</code> channel is closed, and every goroutine waiting on it in a <code>select</code> sees it:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> cook</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">ctx</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> context</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Context</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">id</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">error</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	select</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">After</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Second):</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"dish </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> nil</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">():</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"dish </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> stopped: </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%v\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id, ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Err</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">())</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Err</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	ctx, cancel </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> context.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WithCancel</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(context.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Background</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">())</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">			cook</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(ctx, i)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">500</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">	cancel</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// the customer cancelled</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Three dishes take 2 seconds each, and the customer cancels at 0.5 seconds. All three cooks print <code>context canceled</code> and stop, and the whole run takes 0.5 seconds.</p>
<p><code>context.WithTimeout</code> is the version that cancels itself when time is up. In <code>net/http</code>, every request carries a <code>ctx</code>, <code>r.Context()</code>, which is cancelled when the client disconnects. Pass that <code>ctx</code> down to the work a handler starts, and the work stops along with the request.</p>
<h3>Only three burners: limiting how many run at once</h3>
<p>So far every dish gets its own goroutine. With 5 dishes that's fine, but with 100,000 requests to an external API all sent at once, the other side's rate limit will probably start rejecting them. The kitchen has the same limit: three burners, so only three dishes cook at a time.</p>
<p>The first way is a worker pool: start only three cooks, and have them take orders from one channel.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">dishes </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// no size: it holds no orders, each one goes straight into a cook's hands</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> stove </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; stove </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; stove</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> d </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> range</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> dishes {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">			cookDish</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(d) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// 1 second per dish</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> n </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; n </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	dishes </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> n </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// if no cook is waiting at range, main stops on this line</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">close</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(dishes) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// no more orders; cooks finish what is left and go home</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>The loop at the bottom is the head chef handing out orders 1 to 10, one at a time, into <code>dishes</code>. <code>dishes</code> has no size, so it can't hold a single order. <code>dishes &#x3C;- n</code> only completes when some cook is sitting at <code>range</code> ready to receive. With <code>make(chan int, 10)</code> instead, all 10 orders would go in at once, and the head chef would go straight on to <code>close(dishes)</code> and <code>wg.Wait()</code> without waiting. The cooks still cook at most three dishes at once, though, so it finishes in 4 seconds, the same as the unsized version below. Printing a timestamp where the head chef hands out an order, where a cook takes one, and where a cook goes home gives this order of events:</p>
<pre><code>0 s  head chef hands out 1, 2, 3; each cook takes one and starts
     head chef waits holding 4, since all three cooks are busy
1 s  the three cooks finish and take 4, 5, 6
2 s  they take 7, 8, 9
3 s  one cook takes 10; head chef calls close(dishes)
     the other two cooks' for range gets no more orders, ends, they go home
4 s  the last cook finishes 10 and goes home; wg.Wait() returns
</code></pre>
<p>Which cook gets which order changes from run to run. For the first three seconds three dishes cook at once; in the last second only dish 10 does.</p>
<p>The second way still starts one goroutine per dish, but each has to get a burner before cooking. The burners are a channel with 3 slots, a pattern called a semaphore:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">stoves </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{}, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> d </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; d </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 10</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		stoves </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{}{}        </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// take a burner; wait if all three are taken</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() { </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">stoves }() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// give it back when done</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">		cookDish</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(d)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>With 10 dishes at 1 second each, both versions take 4.0 seconds, with at most 3 dishes cooking at once. 10 dishes split into rounds of 3, 3, 3 and 1, so 4 seconds.</p>
<p>The number of goroutines is different, though. The worker pool has 3 the whole time; the semaphore version starts 10 right away, and 7 of them wait for a burner.</p>
<h3>errgroup: one dish burns, the whole table stops</h3>
<p>A WaitGroup only waits; it doesn't care who failed. But if one dish on a table burns, the whole table is redone, and the other cooks can stop. So as soon as one goroutine returns an error, the others should stop their work, and that error should go back to <code>main</code>.</p>
<p><a href="https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/sync/errgroup"><code>golang.org/x/sync/errgroup</code></a> bundles a WaitGroup, a context and error collection. It is not in the standard library; run <code>go get golang.org/x/sync</code> first.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">g, ctx </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> errgroup.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WithContext</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(context.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Background</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">())</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">g.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">SetLimit</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// three burners</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	g.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">error</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		if</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">==</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">			time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">300</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> errors.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">New</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"dish 2 burned"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		select</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">After</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Second):</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> nil</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">():</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Err</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	})</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">err </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> g.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><code>g.Go</code> is like <code>wg.Go</code>, except the function returns an error. The <code>ctx</code> from <code>errgroup.WithContext</code> is cancelled as soon as the first error comes back; <code>g.SetLimit(3)</code> is the three burners from the previous section; <code>g.Wait()</code> waits for everyone, then returns the first error.</p>
<p>Of 5 dishes, dish 2 burns at 0.3 seconds, and the other cooks see the cancellation on <code>ctx.Done()</code> and stop. <code>g.Wait()</code> returns <code>dish 2 burned</code> after 0.3 seconds.</p>
<h2>Part 3: when it goes wrong</h2>
<p>When these tools are misused, the program either crashes and prints why, or keeps running while some requests never get an answer.</p>
<p>The crash is actually the easier one. It can still take down prod, but at least it leaves a stack trace and a line number. The hang is harder: no crash, no log, no error. Often nobody notices until someone asks in Slack, "hey, is the API ok?"</p>
<h3>Panic</h3>
<p>A panic prints a stack trace the moment it happens, with the line number and the error, so it is easy to find.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">close</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(ch)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D"> // panic: send on closed channel</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> p </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Person</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(p.Name) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// panic: runtime error: invalid memory address or nil pointer dereference</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">a </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> []</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">_ </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> a[</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">5</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">] </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// panic: runtime error: index out of range [5] with length 3</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">interface</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{} </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> "hello"</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">_ </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i.(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// panic: interface conversion: interface {} is string, not int</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>In the kitchen, a panic is a fire at one cook's station. The panic stops that goroutine, runs its <code>defer</code>s, and moves up the call stack. If a deferred function calls <code>recover()</code>, the panic stops there; otherwise the program prints the stack trace and exits.</p>
<p>The trouble is that an unrecovered panic ends the <strong>whole process</strong>, not just that goroutine. A fire at one station sets off the sprinklers in the whole kitchen. And <code>recover()</code> only works in the goroutine that panicked, so <code>main</code> cannot catch a worker's panic. <code>net/http</code> recovers panics in each request handler for you, but goroutines you start with <code>go</code> get nothing, so wrap them yourself:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> goSafe</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">fn</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			if</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> r </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> recover</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(); r </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">!=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> nil</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">				log.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"goroutine panic: </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%v\n%s</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, r, debug.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Stack</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">())</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">			}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">		fn</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<h3>Deadlock</h3>
<p>A deadlock is goroutines waiting on each other, forever.</p>
<p><strong>Channel deadlock.</strong> You already saw one in Part 1. The smallest version is two lines:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// open, unbuffered</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ch </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">              // nobody is receiving, so this blocks forever</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>The same <code>ch &#x3C;- 1</code> panics if the channel is closed. If it is open and nobody receives, it waits forever, as above.</p>
<p><strong>Mutex deadlock.</strong> The most common case is two locks taken in opposite orders. In the kitchen, two cooks grab the wok and the spatula in opposite order:</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wok, spatula </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Mutex</span></span>
