<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() { </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// cook 1</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">100</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// waits for cook 2 to put the spatula down</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() { </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// cook 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">100</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// waits for cook 1 to put the wok down</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Cook 1 holds the wok and waits for the spatula; cook 2 holds the spatula and waits for the wok. Neither lets go. Run on its own, this program is caught too:</p>
<pre><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!

goroutine 1 [sync.WaitGroup.Wait]:
...
goroutine 18 [sync.Mutex.Lock]:
...
goroutine 19 [sync.Mutex.Lock]:
</code></pre>
<h3>When the runtime catches a deadlock, and when it does not</h3>
<p>There are two kinds of stopped goroutine. One is blocked on a channel or a mutex and needs another goroutine to wake it, by receiving a value or calling <code>Unlock()</code>. The other is waiting on the network or a timer, and wakes up by itself when a packet arrives or the time is up.</p>
<p>Go's deadlock detector fires only when <strong>every goroutine is the first kind</strong>, because then nobody can wake anybody. In the kitchen, the manager sees everyone waiting on someone else and closes up. The runtime prints <code>fatal error</code> and where each goroutine is stuck, then ends the program.</p>
<p>A real server never gets there. Some goroutine is always waiting for the next request, which is the second kind, so the runtime never decides the program is stuck. Two cooks are frozen at the stove, but the front of the house is still taking orders, so the manager sees movement and stays out of it.</p>
<p>Each program in the table below has two goroutines blocked forever plus one goroutine that stays alive, and is killed after 6 seconds:</p>

























<table><thead><tr><th>the extra goroutine</th><th>blocked on a channel</th><th>blocked on two mutexes</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>none (<code>main</code> ends in <code>select {}</code>)</td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td></tr><tr><td>a <code>time.Tick</code> loop</td><td>no output until killed</td><td>no output until killed</td></tr><tr><td><code>http.ListenAndServe</code></td><td>no output until killed</td><td>no output until killed</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Only the first row triggers the detector. Rows two and three each have a goroutine waiting on a timer or the network, and the runtime reports nothing. A production server is row three.</p>
<h3>Four ways out</h3>
<p><strong>1. Lock in the same order, always.</strong> The deadlock happens because cook 1 takes the wok first and cook 2 takes the spatula first. If the kitchen rule is that everyone takes the wok first, two cooks can never each hold one tool and wait for the other.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// the rule is the LOCK order: everyone takes wok, then spatula</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// work</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// unlock order is free, reverse is just habit</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Only the order of taking matters; <code>Unlock</code> never blocks, so release in any order. This is simple and correct, but with a dozen locks it gets hard to remember the order. <code>sasha-s/go-deadlock</code> catches wrong orders during development, see the cheat sheet below.</p>
<p><strong>2. Take everything at once.</strong> Wrap both locks as one set and always take them together, like a tool cabinet you open as a whole.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">type</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> Tools</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{ wok, spatula </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Mutex</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> }</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> (</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">t </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Tools</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">LockBoth</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()   { t.wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(); t.spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() }</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> (</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">t </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Tools</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">UnlockBoth</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() { t.spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(); t.wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() }</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>Crude, but it works. A cook who needs only the wok now blocks a cook who needs only the spatula, though. If that is not acceptable, go back to option 1.</p>
<p><strong>3. TryLock with a timeout.</strong> Instead of waiting forever, try for a while and give up.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> tryLock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">m</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Mutex</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">timeout</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> time</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Duration</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">bool</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	ctx, cancel </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> context.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WithTimeout</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(context.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Background</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(), timeout)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> cancel</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		select</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">ctx.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">():</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> false</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D"> // gave up, no deadlock</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		default</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">			if</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> m.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">TryLock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">				return</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> true</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">			}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">			time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p><a href="https://pkg.go.dev/sync#Mutex.TryLock"><code>TryLock</code></a> was added in Go 1.18. It returns <code>true</code> if it gets the lock and <code>false</code> right away if not, without waiting. The <code>context.WithTimeout</code> around it is the timer that decides when to give up. Giving up is not enough: the cook who cannot get the spatula also has to put the wok back so the other cook can finish.</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Lock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	if</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> tryLock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">spatula, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">50</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">time.Millisecond) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">		// cook</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		spatula.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		break</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wok.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Unlock</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// put the wok back</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Duration</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(rand.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Intn</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">100</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> time.Millisecond)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>That last wait has to be random. If both cooks always wait the same time, they come back together and collide again. Both goroutines keep running but make no progress; this is called livelock. Over 10 runs each: with both cooks following the same formula, the cook who finished second needed 4 to 45 attempts, median 14; with a random 0 to 100 ms wait, it took 3 or 4 attempts every time.</p>
<p>The Go documentation is direct about TryLock: "while correct uses of TryLock do exist, they are rare, and use of TryLock is often a sign of a deeper problem in a particular use of mutexes." Use it only when you cannot fix a lock order.</p>
<p><strong>4. Pass messages instead of sharing memory.</strong> From <a href="https://go.dev/doc/effective_go#Share_by_communicating">Effective Go</a>: "Do not communicate by sharing memory; instead, share memory by communicating." Model a tool as a channel with one slot holding one token, like a wok on its hook. <code>select</code> with <code>default</code> means "take it if it is on the hook, otherwise do something else":</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wokHook </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{}, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wokHook </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{}{} </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// the wok is on its hook</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">select</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">case</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> &#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wokHook:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">	// got the wok, cook, then hang it back</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wokHook </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> struct</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">{}{}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">default</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">:</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">	// someone else has it, don't wait</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>With one tool there is no order to get wrong. With more, hooks deadlock just like mutexes: one cook takes the wok, the other takes the spatula, each waits for the second with a plain <code>&#x3C;-hook</code>, and the result is the same <code>fatal error</code>. So option 1's ordering rule still applies.</p>
<h3>Panic and deadlock side by side</h3>



































<table><thead><tr><th></th><th>Panic</th><th>Deadlock</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>When</td><td>immediately</td><td>when goroutines start waiting on each other, which may be rare</td></tr><tr><td>Output</td><td>stack trace, error message</td><td><code>fatal error</code> only when nothing at all can run; in a server, nothing</td></tr><tr><td>Recoverable</td><td>yes, <code>recover()</code>, same goroutine only</td><td>no, restart the process</td></tr><tr><td>Damage</td><td>whole process, unless that goroutine recovers</td><td>requests that need those locks, until restart</td></tr><tr><td>Finding it</td><td>read the error</td><td><code>NumGoroutine</code>, pprof</td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2>Debugging cheat sheet</h2>
<p>Start from the symptom:</p>



































<table><thead><tr><th>What you see</th><th>What it usually is</th><th>First move</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>stack trace, process exits</td><td>unrecovered panic</td><td>read the trace, it names the line</td></tr><tr><td>process dies, trace points into a goroutine you started</td><td>panic with no <code>recover()</code> in that goroutine</td><td>wrap the goroutine (<code>goSafe</code> above)</td></tr><tr><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep</code></td><td>every goroutine blocked</td><td>the trace shows where each one is waiting</td></tr><tr><td>service hangs, no error, goroutine count climbs</td><td>some goroutines blocked forever (mutex or channel)</td><td><code>runtime.NumGoroutine()</code> trend, then pprof</td></tr><tr><td>flaky, occasional garbage data</td><td>data race</td><td><code>go test -race</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>Facts that are easy to get wrong:</p>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>There is no <code>wg.Count()</code>.</strong> To see how much work is left, keep your own <code>atomic.Int64</code> next to the <code>WaitGroup</code>, or watch whether <code>runtime.NumGoroutine()</code> goes up or down.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>pprof for stuck locks:</strong> <code>import _ "net/http/pprof"</code>, start a server (<code>go http.ListenAndServe("localhost:6060", nil)</code>), then open <code>/debug/pprof/goroutine?debug=1</code>. A goroutine waiting for a lock looks like this (file paths shortened):</p>
<pre><code>#	internal/sync.runtime_SemacquireMutex+0x27	runtime/sema.go:95
#	internal/sync.(*Mutex).lockSlow+0x16f		internal/sync/mutex.go:149
#	internal/sync.(*Mutex).Lock+0xd3		internal/sync/mutex.go:70
#	sync.(*Mutex).Lock+0x80				sync/mutex.go:46
#	main.startCooks.func1+0x88			main.go:18
</code></pre>
<p>The <code>main.</code> line at the bottom is the <code>Lock()</code> call in your code that is waiting.</p>
<p>Waiting for a lock does not always mean stuck; many requests may just be queueing. <code>debug=1</code> merges goroutines with the same stack into one entry, so you cannot tell them apart. <code>?debug=2</code> lists each goroutine separately with its ID. Open it twice, 10 seconds apart; the same ID still waiting is probably stuck.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Static checks help little.</strong> <code>go vet</code> catches a copied <code>Mutex</code> or <code>WaitGroup</code>, but it does not report deadlocks.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Catching lock order in development:</strong> replace <code>sync.Mutex</code> with <code>sasha-s/go-deadlock</code>. When it sees two locks taken in opposite orders, it prints a report. Switch back before production.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h2>What to use for what</h2>









































<table><thead><tr><th>What you want</th><th>What to use</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>run at the same time</td><td><code>go</code></td></tr><tr><td>wait for everything to finish</td><td><code>sync.WaitGroup</code>, <code>wg.Go</code></td></tr><tr><td>bring results back</td><td>a channel, <code>close</code> when done sending</td></tr><tr><td>several goroutines change one variable</td><td><code>sync.Mutex</code>; for adding to a number, <code>sync/atomic</code></td></tr><tr><td>wait on several things, set a timeout</td><td><code>select</code>, <code>time.After</code></td></tr><tr><td>stop when told from outside</td><td><code>context</code></td></tr><tr><td>limit how many run at once</td><td>a worker pool, or a buffered channel as a semaphore</td></tr><tr><td>stop everything when one fails</td><td><code>errgroup</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<h2>References</h2>
<ul>
<li>Effective Go, "Share by communicating": <a href="https://go.dev/doc/effective_go#Share_by_communicating">go.dev/doc/effective_go#Share_by_communicating</a></li>
<li>Rob Pike, "Go Proverbs": <a href="https://go-proverbs.github.io/">go-proverbs.github.io</a></li>
<li><code>sync.Mutex.TryLock</code> and <code>sync.WaitGroup.Go</code>: <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/sync">pkg.go.dev/sync</a></li>
<li><code>context</code>: <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/context">pkg.go.dev/context</a></li>
<li><code>errgroup</code>: <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/sync/errgroup">pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/sync/errgroup</a></li>
<li><code>sasha-s/go-deadlock</code>: <a href="https://github.com/sasha-s/go-deadlock">github.com/sasha-s/go-deadlock</a></li>
</ul>15:T6b62,
The simplest way to make Go do many things at once is to put `go` in front of a function call. That is where the problems start, though: `main` doesn't wait for it, the result can't come back, and when several goroutines change the same variable, the count comes out wrong.

One scene runs through the whole article: a restaurant kitchen.

| Kitchen | Go |
|---|---|
| a cook | a goroutine |
| the head chef, who locks up and turns off the lights when leaving | `main` (when `main` returns, the program ends) |
| the counter on the whiteboard: a tick for every dish handed out, crossed off when it is done | `sync.WaitGroup` |
| the pass, the shelf where cooks put finished plates | a channel |
| the "kitchen closed" sign on the pass | `close(ch)` |
| one pen, one wok: while someone uses it, everyone else waits | `sync.Mutex` |
| watching the pass and the kitchen timer at once, and dealing with whichever moves first | `select` |
| the front of house calls in "the customer cancelled", and every cook stops | `context` |
| only three burners, so no matter how many dishes, only three cook at once | a worker pool |
| one dish burns, the whole table is redone, and the other cooks can stop | `errgroup` |
| the kitchen manager: when every cook is waiting on someone else and nobody can move, he closes the kitchen and writes down what each cook was waiting for | the runtime's deadlock detector (prints `fatal error` and where each goroutine is stuck, then ends the program) |

All programs below were run on Go 1.26.1 (darwin/arm64); errgroup is `golang.org/x/sync` v0.20.0.

## Part 1: running things at the same time

### The naive version

Start with the simplest case. Two tasks, one after the other, with sleep standing in for real work.

```go
func doSomething() {
	fmt.Println("Doing something important...")
	time.Sleep(time.Second * 2)
}

func doSomethingElse() {
	fmt.Println("Doing something else, equally important...")
	time.Sleep(time.Second * 3)
}

func main() {
	doSomething()
	doSomethingElse()
}
```

It takes 5 seconds, because the second task waits for the first to finish. One cook, two dishes: finish the first, then start the second.

### "I'll just put go in front of everything"

A goroutine is a function the Go runtime runs concurrently, and starting one is much cheaper than starting an OS thread. So the natural next step is:

```go
func main() {
	go doSomething()
	go doSomethingElse()
	fmt.Println("I'm done")
}
```

```
$ go run main.go
I'm done
```

Out of 1000 runs, 911 printed only `I'm done`. The other 89 printed a line or two more, but neither task finished its sleep.

In the kitchen, the head chef hands out two dishes, says "I'm done", turns off the lights and leaves, and the cooks go home with him wherever they were. When `main` returns, the program ends, and every goroutine still running ends with it.

So how does `main` wait for the goroutines to finish?

### WaitGroup: the counter on the whiteboard

`sync.WaitGroup` is a counter. The head chef puts a tick on the whiteboard for every dish handed out, and each cook crosses one off when done. Until every tick is crossed off, the head chef cannot leave.

- `wg.Add(1)`: add one, one more task.
- `wg.Done()`: subtract one, this task is finished.
- `wg.Wait()`: wait until the counter reaches 0.

```go
func worker(id int, wg *sync.WaitGroup) {
	defer wg.Done()
	fmt.Printf("Worker %d starting\n", id)
	time.Sleep(time.Second)
	fmt.Printf("Worker %d done\n", id)
}

func main() {
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go worker(i, &wg)
	}
	wg.Wait()
	fmt.Println("All workers done")
}
```

Five workers, each sleeping 1 second, finish in 1.0 second total.

Write `Done` with `defer`. Then the counter goes down however the worker returns. Forget it, and one tick stays on the board forever, so `wg.Wait()` waits forever.

Since Go 1.25 you can also use [`wg.Go(f)`](https://pkg.go.dev/sync#WaitGroup.Go), which does the add, the `go`, and the done in one call. Most existing code still uses the form above.

### Channels: when goroutines need to send back results

A WaitGroup tells you *when* everyone is finished, not *what* they produced. For results you need a channel, which is the pass: cooks put plates on it, someone takes them off the other side. `make(chan int, 3)` is a pass with 3 slots. When all slots are full, a cook stands there holding the plate.

```go
func worker(id int, wg *sync.WaitGroup, result chan<- int) {
	defer wg.Done()
	time.Sleep(time.Second * 2)
	result <- id * 2
}

func main() {
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	result := make(chan int, 3)

	for i := 1; i <= 3; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go worker(i, &wg, result)
	}

	go func() {
		wg.Wait()
		close(result)
	}()

	for r := range result {
		fmt.Printf("Received: %d\n", r)
	}
	fmt.Println("All workers done")
}
```

This runs fine. The small `go func()` in the middle has two lines: `wg.Wait()` waits for every cook, and `close(result)` hangs the "kitchen closed" sign.

`close` does not delete or empty the channel. After the sign goes up, nobody can put a new plate on the pass, but plates already there can still be taken. So the `for range` takes the remaining plates and stops once the channel is empty.

Why are those two lines wrapped in `go func()` instead of sitting directly in `main`?

### The first deadlock

Two things keep this program from getting stuck:

- **The `go func()`**: it lets `main` go straight to the `for range`, taking plates off while cooks put them on, so the pass is rarely full.
- **A big enough pass**: 3 slots for 3 workers, so every plate fits even if nobody takes one.

Keep either one and the program finishes:

| `go func()`? | buffer size | result |
|---|---|---|
| yes | 3 | finishes |
| yes | 2 | finishes |
| yes | 0 (unbuffered) | finishes |
| no | 3 | finishes |
| no | 2 | `fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!` |
| no | 0 (unbuffered) | `fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!` |

Remove both:

```go
result := make(chan int, 2) // 3 senders, only 2 slots
// ...
wg.Wait()                     // main blocks here instead of draining
close(result)
for r := range result { ... }
```

This is how it gets stuck:

1. The pass holds 2 plates. The third cook finds it full and stands at `result <- ...` holding the plate.
2. He never reaches `defer wg.Done()`, so his tick is never crossed off.
3. The head chef keeps standing at `wg.Wait()`.
4. The only one who takes plates off the pass is the head chef, and he never reaches the `for range`.
5. Nobody takes a plate, no slot opens, and the third cook keeps standing. Back to step 1.

Everyone is waiting on someone else. The runtime sees that every goroutine is stuck, prints `all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!`, and ends the program.

The fix is to put the `go func()` back. Then `main` starts taking plates right away, and the buffer size stops mattering. A buffer is hard to size to the workload, while taking plates as they arrive always works, so the `go func()` is the safer of the two.

### Mutex: several cooks updating one number

On the kitchen wall is a count of dishes served today. Each cook, after finishing a dish, walks over and adds one. In Go, that is 1000 goroutines each doing `orders++`:

```go
orders := 0
var wg sync.WaitGroup
for i := 0; i < 1000; i++ {
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		orders++
	}()
}
wg.Wait()
fmt.Println(orders)
```

It should end at 1000. Over 30 runs, the result was between 935 and 983, and never 1000.

`orders++` looks like one step but is three: read the current value, add one, write it back. If two cooks both read 41, each adds one and both write 42, and one dish goes uncounted. This is a data race. Run it with `go run -race` and it prints `WARNING: DATA RACE`, plus the two lines that touched the same variable.

The fix is a `sync.Mutex`. After one goroutine calls `Lock()`, anyone else calling `Lock()` waits until the holder calls `Unlock()`. It is like having one pen by the wall: only the cook holding the pen can change the number.

```go
var mu sync.Mutex
// ...
go func() {
	defer wg.Done()
	mu.Lock()
	orders++
	mu.Unlock()
}()
```

With the lock, 10 runs all printed 1000. If all you need is to add to a number, `atomic.Int64` from `sync/atomic` also works: call `Add(1)`, no lock needed.

## Part 2: waiting, cancelling, limiting

### select: waiting on several things at once

So far `main` waits on one thing at a time: `wg.Wait()` for the counter to reach zero, `<-result` for a plate on the pass. Sometimes you need to wait on two things and handle whichever comes first. The most common case is a timeout: the customer waits at most 1 second, and if the dish isn't ready, someone tells them.

```go
func cook(d time.Duration) <-chan string {
	ch := make(chan string, 1)
	go func() {
		time.Sleep(d)
		ch <- "steak"
	}()
	return ch
}

func main() {
	select {
	case dish := <-cook(2 * time.Second):
		fmt.Println("got", dish)
	case <-time.After(1 * time.Second):
		fmt.Println("timeout")
	}
}
```

`select` blocks until one of its cases has a value. The steak takes 2 seconds and `time.After(1 * time.Second)` sends a value after 1 second, so this prints `timeout` after 1.0 seconds.

The channel in `cook` has 1 slot because nobody will take the steak after the timeout. With no slot, that cook would stand holding the plate forever, and the goroutine would never end.

`select` can also have a `default`. If no channel is ready, it takes `default` and doesn't wait.

### context: the customer cancelled

A timeout stops waiting when time runs out. Sometimes the signal comes from outside instead, "no need to finish": the user closes the page and the HTTP request is cancelled. Cooks still working on it should stop too, or the work is wasted.

`context` carries that message. `context.WithCancel` gives you a `ctx` and a `cancel` function. After `cancel()`, the `ctx.Done()` channel is closed, and every goroutine waiting on it in a `select` sees it:

```go
func cook(ctx context.Context, id int) error {
	select {
	case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
		fmt.Printf("dish %d done\n", id)
		return nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		fmt.Printf("dish %d stopped: %v\n", id, ctx.Err())
		return ctx.Err()
	}
}

func main() {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := 1; i <= 3; i++ {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			cook(ctx, i)
		}()
	}
	time.Sleep(500 * time.Millisecond)
	cancel() // the customer cancelled
	wg.Wait()
}
```

Three dishes take 2 seconds each, and the customer cancels at 0.5 seconds. All three cooks print `context canceled` and stop, and the whole run takes 0.5 seconds.

`context.WithTimeout` is the version that cancels itself when time is up. In `net/http`, every request carries a `ctx`, `r.Context()`, which is cancelled when the client disconnects. Pass that `ctx` down to the work a handler starts, and the work stops along with the request.

### Only three burners: limiting how many run at once

So far every dish gets its own goroutine. With 5 dishes that's fine, but with 100,000 requests to an external API all sent at once, the other side's rate limit will probably start rejecting them. The kitchen has the same limit: three burners, so only three dishes cook at a time.

The first way is a worker pool: start only three cooks, and have them take orders from one channel.

```go
dishes := make(chan int) // no size: it holds no orders, each one goes straight into a cook's hands
var wg sync.WaitGroup
for stove := 1; stove <= 3; stove++ {
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		for d := range dishes {
			cookDish(d) // 1 second per dish
		}
	}()
}
for n := 1; n <= 10; n++ {
	dishes <- n // if no cook is waiting at range, main stops on this line
}
close(dishes) // no more orders; cooks finish what is left and go home
wg.Wait()
```

The loop at the bottom is the head chef handing out orders 1 to 10, one at a time, into `dishes`. `dishes` has no size, so it can't hold a single order. `dishes <- n` only completes when some cook is sitting at `range` ready to receive. With `make(chan int, 10)` instead, all 10 orders would go in at once, and the head chef would go straight on to `close(dishes)` and `wg.Wait()` without waiting. The cooks still cook at most three dishes at once, though, so it finishes in 4 seconds, the same as the unsized version below. Printing a timestamp where the head chef hands out an order, where a cook takes one, and where a cook goes home gives this order of events:

```
0 s  head chef hands out 1, 2, 3; each cook takes one and starts
     head chef waits holding 4, since all three cooks are busy
1 s  the three cooks finish and take 4, 5, 6
2 s  they take 7, 8, 9
3 s  one cook takes 10; head chef calls close(dishes)
     the other two cooks' for range gets no more orders, ends, they go home
4 s  the last cook finishes 10 and goes home; wg.Wait() returns
```

Which cook gets which order changes from run to run. For the first three seconds three dishes cook at once; in the last second only dish 10 does.

The second way still starts one goroutine per dish, but each has to get a burner before cooking. The burners are a channel with 3 slots, a pattern called a semaphore:

```go
stoves := make(chan struct{}, 3)
for d := 1; d <= 10; d++ {
	wg.Add(1)
	go func() {
		defer wg.Done()
		stoves <- struct{}{}        // take a burner; wait if all three are taken
		defer func() { <-stoves }() // give it back when done
		cookDish(d)
	}()
}
wg.Wait()
```

With 10 dishes at 1 second each, both versions take 4.0 seconds, with at most 3 dishes cooking at once. 10 dishes split into rounds of 3, 3, 3 and 1, so 4 seconds.

The number of goroutines is different, though. The worker pool has 3 the whole time; the semaphore version starts 10 right away, and 7 of them wait for a burner.

### errgroup: one dish burns, the whole table stops

A WaitGroup only waits; it doesn't care who failed. But if one dish on a table burns, the whole table is redone, and the other cooks can stop. So as soon as one goroutine returns an error, the others should stop their work, and that error should go back to `main`.

[`golang.org/x/sync/errgroup`](https://pkg.go.dev/golang.org/x/sync/errgroup) bundles a WaitGroup, a context and error collection. It is not in the standard library; run `go get golang.org/x/sync` first.

```go
g, ctx := errgroup.WithContext(context.Background())
g.SetLimit(3) // three burners
for i := 1; i <= 5; i++ {
	g.Go(func() error {
		if i == 2 {
			time.Sleep(300 * time.Millisecond)
			return errors.New("dish 2 burned")
		}
		select {
		case <-time.After(2 * time.Second):
			return nil
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return ctx.Err()
		}
	})
}
err := g.Wait()
```

`g.Go` is like `wg.Go`, except the function returns an error. The `ctx` from `errgroup.WithContext` is cancelled as soon as the first error comes back; `g.SetLimit(3)` is the three burners from the previous section; `g.Wait()` waits for everyone, then returns the first error.

Of 5 dishes, dish 2 burns at 0.3 seconds, and the other cooks see the cancellation on `ctx.Done()` and stop. `g.Wait()` returns `dish 2 burned` after 0.3 seconds.

## Part 3: when it goes wrong

When these tools are misused, the program either crashes and prints why, or keeps running while some requests never get an answer.

The crash is actually the easier one. It can still take down prod, but at least it leaves a stack trace and a line number. The hang is harder: no crash, no log, no error. Often nobody notices until someone asks in Slack, "hey, is the API ok?"

### Panic

A panic prints a stack trace the moment it happens, with the line number and the error, so it is easy to find.

```go
ch := make(chan int)
close(ch)
ch <- 1 // panic: send on closed channel

var p *Person
fmt.Println(p.Name) // panic: runtime error: invalid memory address or nil pointer dereference

a := []int{1, 2, 3}
_ = a[5] // panic: runtime error: index out of range [5] with length 3

var i interface{} = "hello"
_ = i.(int) // panic: interface conversion: interface {} is string, not int
```

In the kitchen, a panic is a fire at one cook's station. The panic stops that goroutine, runs its `defer`s, and moves up the call stack. If a deferred function calls `recover()`, the panic stops there; otherwise the program prints the stack trace and exits.

The trouble is that an unrecovered panic ends the **whole process**, not just that goroutine. A fire at one station sets off the sprinklers in the whole kitchen. And `recover()` only works in the goroutine that panicked, so `main` cannot catch a worker's panic. `net/http` recovers panics in each request handler for you, but goroutines you start with `go` get nothing, so wrap them yourself:

```go
func goSafe(fn func()) {
	go func() {
		defer func() {
			if r := recover(); r != nil {
				log.Printf("goroutine panic: %v\n%s", r, debug.Stack())
			}
		}()
		fn()
	}()
}
```

### Deadlock

A deadlock is goroutines waiting on each other, forever.

**Channel deadlock.** You already saw one in Part 1. The smallest version is two lines:

```go
ch := make(chan int) // open, unbuffered
ch <- 1              // nobody is receiving, so this blocks forever
// fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!
```

The same `ch <- 1` panics if the channel is closed. If it is open and nobody receives, it waits forever, as above.

**Mutex deadlock.** The most common case is two locks taken in opposite orders. In the kitchen, two cooks grab the wok and the spatula in opposite order:

```go
func main() {
	var wok, spatula sync.Mutex
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	wg.Add(2)

	go func() { // cook 1
		defer wg.Done()
		wok.Lock()
		time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
		spatula.Lock() // waits for cook 2 to put the spatula down
		spatula.Unlock()
		wok.Unlock()
	}()

	go func() { // cook 2
		defer wg.Done()
		spatula.Lock()
		time.Sleep(100 * time.Millisecond)
		wok.Lock() // waits for cook 1 to put the wok down
		wok.Unlock()
		spatula.Unlock()
	}()

	wg.Wait()
}
```

Cook 1 holds the wok and waits for the spatula; cook 2 holds the spatula and waits for the wok. Neither lets go. Run on its own, this program is caught too:

```
fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!

goroutine 1 [sync.WaitGroup.Wait]:
...
goroutine 18 [sync.Mutex.Lock]:
...
goroutine 19 [sync.Mutex.Lock]:
```

### When the runtime catches a deadlock, and when it does not

There are two kinds of stopped goroutine. One is blocked on a channel or a mutex and needs another goroutine to wake it, by receiving a value or calling `Unlock()`. The other is waiting on the network or a timer, and wakes up by itself when a packet arrives or the time is up.

Go's deadlock detector fires only when **every goroutine is the first kind**, because then nobody can wake anybody. In the kitchen, the manager sees everyone waiting on someone else and closes up. The runtime prints `fatal error` and where each goroutine is stuck, then ends the program.

A real server never gets there. Some goroutine is always waiting for the next request, which is the second kind, so the runtime never decides the program is stuck. Two cooks are frozen at the stove, but the front of the house is still taking orders, so the manager sees movement and stays out of it.

Each program in the table below has two goroutines blocked forever plus one goroutine that stays alive, and is killed after 6 seconds:

| the extra goroutine | blocked on a channel | blocked on two mutexes |
|---|---|---|
| none (`main` ends in `select {}`) | `fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!` | `fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!` |
| a `time.Tick` loop | no output until killed | no output until killed |
| `http.ListenAndServe` | no output until killed | no output until killed |

Only the first row triggers the detector. Rows two and three each have a goroutine waiting on a timer or the network, and the runtime reports nothing. A production server is row three.

### Four ways out

**1. Lock in the same order, always.** The deadlock happens because cook 1 takes the wok first and cook 2 takes the spatula first. If the kitchen rule is that everyone takes the wok first, two cooks can never each hold one tool and wait for the other.

```go
// the rule is the LOCK order: everyone takes wok, then spatula
wok.Lock()
spatula.Lock()
// work
spatula.Unlock() // unlock order is free, reverse is just habit
wok.Unlock()
```

Only the order of taking matters; `Unlock` never blocks, so release in any order. This is simple and correct, but with a dozen locks it gets hard to remember the order. `sasha-s/go-deadlock` catches wrong orders during development, see the cheat sheet below.

**2. Take everything at once.** Wrap both locks as one set and always take them together, like a tool cabinet you open as a whole.

```go
type Tools struct{ wok, spatula sync.Mutex }

func (t *Tools) LockBoth()   { t.wok.Lock(); t.spatula.Lock() }
func (t *Tools) UnlockBoth() { t.spatula.Unlock(); t.wok.Unlock() }
```

Crude, but it works. A cook who needs only the wok now blocks a cook who needs only the spatula, though. If that is not acceptable, go back to option 1.

**3. TryLock with a timeout.** Instead of waiting forever, try for a while and give up.

```go
func tryLock(m *sync.Mutex, timeout time.Duration) bool {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), timeout)
	defer cancel()
	for {
		select {
		case <-ctx.Done():
			return false // gave up, no deadlock
		default:
			if m.TryLock() {
				return true
			}
			time.Sleep(time.Millisecond)
		}
	}
}
```

[`TryLock`](https://pkg.go.dev/sync#Mutex.TryLock) was added in Go 1.18. It returns `true` if it gets the lock and `false` right away if not, without waiting. The `context.WithTimeout` around it is the timer that decides when to give up. Giving up is not enough: the cook who cannot get the spatula also has to put the wok back so the other cook can finish.

```go
for {
	wok.Lock()
	if tryLock(&spatula, 50*time.Millisecond) {
		// cook
		spatula.Unlock()
		wok.Unlock()
		break
	}
	wok.Unlock() // put the wok back
	time.Sleep(time.Duration(rand.Intn(100)) * time.Millisecond)
}
```

That last wait has to be random. If both cooks always wait the same time, they come back together and collide again. Both goroutines keep running but make no progress; this is called livelock. Over 10 runs each: with both cooks following the same formula, the cook who finished second needed 4 to 45 attempts, median 14; with a random 0 to 100 ms wait, it took 3 or 4 attempts every time.

The Go documentation is direct about TryLock: "while correct uses of TryLock do exist, they are rare, and use of TryLock is often a sign of a deeper problem in a particular use of mutexes." Use it only when you cannot fix a lock order.

**4. Pass messages instead of sharing memory.** From [Effective Go](https://go.dev/doc/effective_go#Share_by_communicating): "Do not communicate by sharing memory; instead, share memory by communicating." Model a tool as a channel with one slot holding one token, like a wok on its hook. `select` with `default` means "take it if it is on the hook, otherwise do something else":

```go
wokHook := make(chan struct{}, 1)
wokHook <- struct{}{} // the wok is on its hook

select {
case <-wokHook:
	// got the wok, cook, then hang it back
	wokHook <- struct{}{}
default:
	// someone else has it, don't wait
}
```

With one tool there is no order to get wrong. With more, hooks deadlock just like mutexes: one cook takes the wok, the other takes the spatula, each waits for the second with a plain `<-hook`, and the result is the same `fatal error`. So option 1's ordering rule still applies.

### Panic and deadlock side by side

| | Panic | Deadlock |
|---|---|---|
| When | immediately | when goroutines start waiting on each other, which may be rare |
| Output | stack trace, error message | `fatal error` only when nothing at all can run; in a server, nothing |
| Recoverable | yes, `recover()`, same goroutine only | no, restart the process |
| Damage | whole process, unless that goroutine recovers | requests that need those locks, until restart |
| Finding it | read the error | `NumGoroutine`, pprof |

## Debugging cheat sheet

Start from the symptom:

| What you see | What it usually is | First move |
|---|---|---|
| stack trace, process exits | unrecovered panic | read the trace, it names the line |
| process dies, trace points into a goroutine you started | panic with no `recover()` in that goroutine | wrap the goroutine (`goSafe` above) |
| `fatal error: all goroutines are asleep` | every goroutine blocked | the trace shows where each one is waiting |
| service hangs, no error, goroutine count climbs | some goroutines blocked forever (mutex or channel) | `runtime.NumGoroutine()` trend, then pprof |
| flaky, occasional garbage data | data race | `go test -race` |

Facts that are easy to get wrong:

- **There is no `wg.Count()`.** To see how much work is left, keep your own `atomic.Int64` next to the `WaitGroup`, or watch whether `runtime.NumGoroutine()` goes up or down.
- **pprof for stuck locks:** `import _ "net/http/pprof"`, start a server (`go http.ListenAndServe("localhost:6060", nil)`), then open `/debug/pprof/goroutine?debug=1`. A goroutine waiting for a lock looks like this (file paths shortened):

  ```
  #	internal/sync.runtime_SemacquireMutex+0x27	runtime/sema.go:95
  #	internal/sync.(*Mutex).lockSlow+0x16f		internal/sync/mutex.go:149
  #	internal/sync.(*Mutex).Lock+0xd3		internal/sync/mutex.go:70
  #	sync.(*Mutex).Lock+0x80				sync/mutex.go:46
  #	main.startCooks.func1+0x88			main.go:18
  ```

  The `main.` line at the bottom is the `Lock()` call in your code that is waiting.

  Waiting for a lock does not always mean stuck; many requests may just be queueing. `debug=1` merges goroutines with the same stack into one entry, so you cannot tell them apart. `?debug=2` lists each goroutine separately with its ID. Open it twice, 10 seconds apart; the same ID still waiting is probably stuck.
- **Static checks help little.** `go vet` catches a copied `Mutex` or `WaitGroup`, but it does not report deadlocks.
- **Catching lock order in development:** replace `sync.Mutex` with `sasha-s/go-deadlock`. When it sees two locks taken in opposite orders, it prints a report. Switch back before production.

## What to use for what

| What you want | What to use |
|---|---|
| run at the same time | `go` |
| wait for everything to finish | `sync.WaitGroup`, `wg.Go` |
| bring results back | a channel, `close` when done sending |
| several goroutines change one variable | `sync.Mutex`; for adding to a number, `sync/atomic` |
| wait on several things, set a timeout | `select`, `time.After` |
| stop when told from outside | `context` |
| limit how many run at once | a worker pool, or a buffered channel as a semaphore |
| stop everything when one fails | `errgroup` |

要一次做很多事，最簡單的寫法是在函式前面加一個 <code>go</code>。不過加了以後，問題才開始冒出來：<code>main</code> 不會等它做完，做出來的東西也拿不回來，好幾個 goroutine 改同一個變數，數字還會算錯。</p>
<p>整篇用同一個場景：一間餐廳的廚房。</p>





















































<table><thead><tr><th>廚房</th><th>Go</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>廚師</td><td>goroutine</td></tr><tr><td>主廚，他離開時會關燈鎖門</td><td><code>main</code>（<code>main</code> return 了，整個程式就結束）</td></tr><tr><td>白板上的 counter：交代一道菜就打一個勾，做完一道就劃掉一個</td><td><code>sync.WaitGroup</code></td></tr><tr><td>出菜口，廚師把做好的盤子放在這裡</td><td>channel</td></tr><tr><td>出菜口掛上「廚房打烊」的牌子</td><td><code>close(ch)</code></td></tr><tr><td>只有一支筆、一個炒鍋，有人在用，別人就得等</td><td><code>sync.Mutex</code></td></tr><tr><td>同時盯著出菜口跟牆上的計時器，哪邊先有動靜就先處理哪邊</td><td><code>select</code></td></tr><tr><td>外場傳話進來「客人取消了」，每個廚師聽到就停手</td><td><code>context</code></td></tr><tr><td>只有三個爐子，菜再多也只能三道一起煮</td><td>worker pool</td></tr><tr><td>一道菜燒焦，整桌重做，其他廚師也不用做了</td><td><code>errgroup</code></td></tr><tr><td>廚房經理：發現每個人都在等別人、誰也動不了，就宣布打烊，順便記下每個人在等什麼</td><td>runtime 的 deadlock 偵測（印出 <code>fatal error</code> 跟每個 goroutine 停在哪裡，然後結束程式）</td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>下面的程式都用 Go 1.26.1（darwin/arm64）跑過，errgroup 用的是 <code>golang.org/x/sync</code> v0.20.0。</p>
<h2>第一部分：讓程式同時跑</h2>
<h3>最直接的寫法</h3>
<p>先從最簡單的開始吧。兩件事，做完一件再做下一件，用 sleep 假裝在忙。</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Doing something important..."</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Doing something else, equally important..."</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">	doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">	doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>跑完要 5 秒，因為第二件事得等第一件做完才開始。就像一個廚師做兩道菜，做完一道再接著做下一道。</p>
<h3>「那每個函式前面都加 go 就好了吧」</h3>
<p>goroutine 是 Go runtime 同時執行的函式，開一個比開一條 OS thread 便宜很多。所以很自然會想這樣寫：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomething</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> doSomethingElse</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"I'm done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<pre><code>$ go run main.go
I'm done
</code></pre>
<p>跑 1000 次，有 911 次只印出 <code>I'm done</code>。剩下的 89 次多印了一兩行，不過兩件事都沒等到 sleep 結束。</p>
<p>換成廚房的話，就是主廚把兩道菜交代下去，說一聲「I'm done」就關燈走人了，廚師不管做到哪都只能跟著下班。<code>main</code> 一 return，程式就結束，還在跑的 goroutine 也一起結束。</p>
<p>那 <code>main</code> 要怎麼等 goroutine 做完呢？</p>
<h3>WaitGroup：白板上的 counter</h3>
<p><code>sync.WaitGroup</code> 就是一個 counter。主廚每交代一道菜，就在白板上打一個勾；廚師做完一道，就劃掉一個勾。勾還沒全部劃掉，主廚就不能走。</p>
<ul>
<li><code>wg.Add(1)</code>：加一，多一件要做。</li>
<li><code>wg.Done()</code>：減一，這件做完了。</li>
<li><code>wg.Wait()</code>：等到 counter 變成 0 才往下走。</li>
</ul>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">id</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">wg</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Worker </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> starting</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Worker </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62"> done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, id)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 5</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(i, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"All workers done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>五個 worker 各 sleep 1 秒，全部跑完一共 1.0 秒。</p>
<p><code>Done</code> 最好用 <code>defer</code> 寫。這樣不管 worker 從哪裡 return，counter 都會減一。要是忘了寫，白板上就會一直留著一個沒劃掉的勾，<code>wg.Wait()</code> 也就永遠等下去。</p>
<p>Go 1.25 之後也可以改用 <a href="https://pkg.go.dev/sync#WaitGroup.Go"><code>wg.Go(f)</code></a>，它把加一、開 goroutine、減一這三件事一次做完。不過現有的程式碼大多還是上面那種寫法。</p>
<h3>Channel：goroutine 要把結果交回來的時候</h3>
<p>WaitGroup 只知道大家<em>什麼時候</em>做完，不知道大家<em>做出了什麼</em>。要把結果帶回來，就得用 channel，也就是出菜口：廚師把盤子放上去，有人從另一邊拿走。<code>make(chan int, 3)</code> 是一個有 3 個空位的出菜口。空位滿了的話，廚師就只能端著盤子站在那裡等。</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">id</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">wg</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> *</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#FFAB70;--shiki-light:#E36209">result</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> chan&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	defer</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Done</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	time.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Sleep</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(time.Second </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;-</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> id </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">*</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 2</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> main</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	var</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> wg </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">sync</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">WaitGroup</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x3C;=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5"> 3</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">; i</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">++</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Add</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">1</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">		go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> worker</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(i, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">&#x26;</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg, result)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	go</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> func</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">() {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">		close</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(result)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}()</span></span>
<span data-line=""> </span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">	for</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> r </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> range</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E"> result {</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">		fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Printf</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"Received: </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">%d\n</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, r)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	}</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">	fmt.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Println</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF;--shiki-light:#032F62">"All workers done"</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">)</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">}</span></span></code></pre></figure>
<p>這樣可以正常跑完。中間那個小小的 <code>go func()</code> 裡面有兩行：<code>wg.Wait()</code> 等所有廚師做完，<code>close(result)</code> 掛上「廚房打烊」的牌子。</p>
<p><code>close</code> 不會刪掉 channel，也不會清空它。牌子掛上去之後，只是不能再放新的盤子，已經放上去的照樣拿得到。所以 <code>for range</code> 會把剩下的盤子拿完，channel 空了才停。</p>
<p>問題是，這兩行為什麼要包在 <code>go func()</code> 裡，不直接寫在 <code>main</code> 呢？</p>
<h3>第一個 deadlock</h3>
<p>有兩個地方讓這段程式不會卡住：</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>go func()</code></strong>：有了它，<code>main</code> 可以直接進到 <code>for range</code>，廚師一邊放、<code>main</code> 一邊拿，出菜口很少會滿。</li>
<li><strong>出菜口夠大</strong>：3 個空位、3 個 worker，就算一直沒人來拿，每個盤子也放得下。</li>
</ul>
<p>兩個只要留一個，程式就會正常結束：</p>








































<table><thead><tr><th>有沒有 <code>go func()</code></th><th>buffer 大小</th><th>結果</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>有</td><td>3</td><td>正常結束</td></tr><tr><td>有</td><td>2</td><td>正常結束</td></tr><tr><td>有</td><td>0（unbuffered）</td><td>正常結束</td></tr><tr><td>沒有</td><td>3</td><td>正常結束</td></tr><tr><td>沒有</td><td>2</td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td></tr><tr><td>沒有</td><td>0（unbuffered）</td><td><code>fatal error: all goroutines are asleep - deadlock!</code></td></tr></tbody></table>
<p>兩個都拿掉的話：</p>
<figure data-rehype-pretty-code-figure=""><pre tabindex="0" data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light"><code data-language="go" data-theme="github-dark github-light" style="display: grid;"><span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">result </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">:=</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1"> make</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">(</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49">chan</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#F97583;--shiki-light:#D73A49"> int</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">, </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#79B8FF;--shiki-light:#005CC5">2</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">) </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// 3 個 sender，只有 2 個空位</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// ...</span></span>
<span data-line=""><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">wg.</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#B392F0;--shiki-light:#6F42C1">Wait</span><span style="--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8;--shiki-light:#24292E">()                     </span><span style="--shiki-dark:#6A737D;--shiki-light:#6A737D">// main 停在這裡，沒去拿結果</span></span>
